THE CITY · Qwen 27B, rules v2, 400 days, game 1
Relief for anyone with no food passes 20 to 0 on day 10, and a 10% tax two days later. The store hands out 1,116 food, nobody dies, and 2,080 food are left at the end.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 1 · qwen
